# Every name string the S-03 verifiers typed or generated in rounds 1-4 (October 2026), one per line, backslash-escaped (\\ \t \n);
# a line starting with "#" is a comment. Sources (scratch folders of the verifier rounds): atk-legit-r1..r3 names.txt, names2.txt,
# ui_names.txt, more.txt, seed_names.txt and rule_names*.txt (real names, typed at sign-up and in Edit Profile, EN/AR, 390/1440);
# atk-unicode-r2 scan1/scan2 (letters and letter + mark units that drew alike); atk-unicode-r3 (a mark over an undotted Arabic
# letter: «حْالد» / «خالد» …). supabase/tests/name_rule.sql checks that the database gives what the live app gives for each one
# (node tools/names/name_rule.mjs --strings), and that no allowed name keeps a combining mark.
